Volume - 157 BENGALURU, MONDAY, 19, DECEMBER, 2022(MARGASHIRA, 28, SHAKAVARSHA, 1944 Issue 255

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
No. DPAR 12 SRC 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Vidhana Soudha
Bengaluru, Dated: 16.12.2022

NOTIFICATION
The draft of the following rules further to amend the Karnataka State Civil
Services (Conduct) Rules, 2021, which the Government of Karnataka proposes to
make in exercise of the powers conferred by sub - section (1) of section 3 read with
section 8 of the Karnataka State Civil Services Act, 1978 (Karnataka Act 14 of 1990)
is hereby published as required by sub-section (2) of section 3 of the said Act for the
information of all persons likely to be affected thereby and notice is hereby given that
the said draft will be taken in to consideration after fifteen days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any person with respect to the said draft before expiry of the period specified
above, will be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestions may be
addressed to the Secretary to Government, Department of Personnel and
Administrative Reforms, Vidhana Soudha, Bengaluru – 560001.

DRAFT RULES

1. Title and Commencement,- (1) These rules may be called the Karnataka State
Civil Services (Conduct) (Amendment) Rules, 2022.
(20458)

won 2 BF RE MAW, Aamo, OC, @Fowo’, 9099 SOOBE
(2) They shall come into force from the date of their final publication in the Official
Gazette.

2. Amendment of Rule 24,- In the Karnataka State Civil Services (Conduct) Rules,
2021, in rule 24, in sub-rule (2), for the figures and word
“31st December”, the figures and word “31st march”, shall be substituted.

By Order and in the name of the
Governor of Karnataka

(Nagarathna V. Patil)
Under Secretary to Government
Department of Personnel and
Administrative Reforms
(Service Rules-2)

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 214 PFH 2022
Dated:11-10-2022. regarding altering the limits of Maranal village of Hunasagi
Taluk and Yadgir District and constituting a new revenue village called Mailapur
was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 214 PFH 2022
dated:13-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
dated:13-10-2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Maranal of Hunasagi taluk in Yadagiri District to form a new revenue
village called Mailapur of Hunsagi taluk Yadagiri district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 291 PFH 2022 Dated:11-10-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Miriyan village of Chincholi taluk, Kalaburagi
District and constituting a village called Kristapur and Kristapur Tanda was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 291 PFH 2022
dated:14-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:
14-10-2022.

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:

SCHEDULE-1

Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
as specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Miriyan of Chincholi taluk, Kalaburgi District to form a new revenue
village called as Kristapur of Chincholi taluk and Kalaburgi District along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Date:15-12-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 219 PFH 2022
Dated:11-10-2022 regarding altering the limits of Narayanpura village of Hunasagi
Taluk and Yadgir District and constituting a new revenue village called
Hanumanagara was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD
219 PFH 2022 dated:13-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
dated:13-10-2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Narayanpura of Hunasagi taluk in Yadagiri District to form a new revenue
village called Hanumanagara of Hunsagi taluk Yadagiri district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 301 PFH 2022 dated:14-10-2022 inviting

objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within

here by makes the following notification, namely:

villages Ranapur of Chincholi taluk, Kalaburagi District to form a new revenue

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 292 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date: 15-12-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 292 PFH 2022 Dated:13-10-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Salebernalli village of Chincholi taluk,
Kalaburagi District and constituting a village called Janglipeera Tanda was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 292 PFH 2022
dated:14-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated: 14-
10-2022.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:

SCHEDULE-1

Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
as specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Salebernalli of Chincholi taluk, Kalaburagi District to form a new revenue
village called as Janglipeera Tanda of Chincholi taluk and Kalaburagi District
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 300 PFH 2022 Dated:13-10-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Shadipur village of Chincholi taluk,
Kalaburagi District and constituting a village called of Chincholi taluk, Kalaburagi
district to constitute a new village Chaplanayak nagar, Bhikkunayak nagar,
Chandunayak nagar, Dhansinghnayak nagar and Javaharnayak nagar was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 300 PFH 2022
dated:14-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:
14-10-2022.

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:

SCHEDULE-1

Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
as specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Shadipur of Chincholi taluk, Kalaburagi District to form a new revenue
village called as Chaplanayak nagar of Chincholi taluk and Kalaburagi District
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 277 PFH 2022 Dated:13-10-2022.
regarding altering the limits of Shakhapur village of Shahapur Taluk and Yadgir
District and constituting a new revenue village called Shakhapur Sevanagar was

PR-1366

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 225 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Date:15-12-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 225 PFH 2022 Dated:. 11-10-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Talavaragera village of Shorapur Taluk and
Yadgir District and constituting a new revenue village called Mallibhavi was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 225 PFH 2022
dated:13-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
dated:13-10-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Talavaragera of Shorapur taluk in Yadagiri District to form a new revenue
village called Mallibhavi of Shorapur taluk Yadagiri district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1367
GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 279 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Date:15-12-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 279 PFH 2022 Dated:10-10-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Vadagera village of Vadagera Taluk and
Yadagiri District and constituting a new revenue village called Biranakal,
Basavanagar And Gopalnayak Nagar was published as required by section 6 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka

Gazette No. RD 279 PFH 2022 dated:14-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions
from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:14-10-
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Vadagera of Vadagera taluk in Yadagiri District to form a new revenue
village called Biranakal of Vadagera taluk Yadagiri district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof

Boundaries of the village Vadagera

East: Konahalli Village Boundary. West: Ikur, Basava Nagar and
Biranakal new proposed Revenue
Village Border.
North: Tekaral, Horatur, Halgera And South: Tumakur, Kurihal and Basava
Ullesugur Village Boundary. Nagar new proposed Revenue Village
Boundary.

The above shall come into effect from date of publication of final notification
in the official gazette.

By order in the name of the
Governor of Karnataka

(Dr. Rajendra Prasad M. N.,)
Director, Cell for creation of revenue villages
and Ex-officio Joint Secretary to Govt.
Revenue Department

PR-1368
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 209 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:15-12-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 209 GLH 2022 Dated:13-
10-2022 regarding altering the limits of Venkatapur village of Chincholi taluk,
Kalaburagi District and constituting a village called Gopunayak nagar was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 209 GLH 2022
dated:14-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:
14-10-2022.

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:

SCHEDULE-1

Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
as specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Venkatapur of Chincholi taluk, Kalaburagi District to form a new revenue
village called as Gopunayak nagar of Chincholi taluk and Kalaburagi District
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 220 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Date:15-12-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 220 PFH 2022
Dated:11-10-2022 regarding altering the limits of Yarakihal village of Hunasagi
Taluk and Yadgir District and constituting a new revenue village called
Dharmanagara was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD
220 PFH 2022 dated:13-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
dated:13-10-2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Yarakihal of Hunasagi taluk in Yadagiri District to form a new revenue
village called Dharmanagara of Hunsagi taluk Yadagiri district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1370

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 252 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Dated: 15-12-2022.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 252 PFH 2022 Dated: 11-10-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Harangera village Shahapur Taluk Yadgir
District and constituting a new revenue village called Maddinagar &
Lokanayaknagar was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD
252 PFH 2022 dated:12-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated: 14-10-
2022.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-I
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Harangera of Shahapur Taluk in Yadgir district to form the a new revenue
village called Maddinagar of Shahapur Taluk, Yadgir district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 254 PFH 2022 Dated: 11-10-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Hosakera village of Shahapur taluk Yadgir
District and constituting a new revenue village called Devalunayak Tanda &
Balajinagar was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 254 PFH
2022 dated:14-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely
to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated. 14-10-
2022.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-I
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Hosakera of Shahapur Taluk in Yadgir district to form the a new revenue
village called Devalunayak Tanda of Shahapur Taluk,Yadgir district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
No. RD 249 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretaraiat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Dated:15-12-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 249 PFH 2022 Dated: 06-09-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Rajapur (K) village of Shahapur Taluk Yadgir
District and constituting a new revenue village called Doulatinayak nagar was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 249 PFH 2022
dated:14-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated. 14-10-
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-I
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Rajapur (K) of Shahapur Taluk inYadgir district to form the a new revenue
village called Doulatinayak nagar of Shahapur Taluk, Yadgir district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official

village Sagar of Shahapur Taluk inYadgir district to form the a new revenue village
